# Usage: sbatch run_exp1_v2.sh Qwen3-0.6B Qwen3-1.7B# Runs experiment 1 on each listed model, one after another, inside a single job.

===== script ====="""Experiment 1: measure the knowledge-utilization (compositionality) gap.
For every 2-hop question in Compositional Celebrities we ask the model:  q1   - first hop alone            ("What is the birthplace of Rumi?")  q2   - second hop alone           ("What is the capital of Afghanistan?")  comp - the composed question      ("What is the capital of the birthplace of Rumi?")  ctx  - composed question with the first-hop fact given in context
Gap = share of items where the model gets BOTH hops right on their own      but still gets the composed question wrong.
Usage:  python exp1_gap.py --model models/Qwen3-0.6B --out results/exp1_Qwen3-0.6B  python exp1_gap.py --dry-run          # logic test without a GPU"""import argparseimport jsonimport osimport randomimport reimport timeimport unicodedatafrom collections import defaultdict
SYSTEM = "Answer the question with only the answer itself: a name, a number, or a short phrase. No explanation."
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- scoringdef norm(s):    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", str(s)).lower()    s = re.sub(r"<think>.*?</think>", " ", s, flags=re.S)    s = s.replace("+", " ")    s = re.sub(r"[^\w\s]", " ", s)          # drop punctuation, keep all scripts    s = re.sub(r"\b(the|a|an)\b", " ", s)    s = re.sub(r"\b0+(\d)", r"\1", s)        # 004 -> 4    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s).strip()
def is_correct(pred, aliases):    p = norm(pred)    for a in aliases:        raw = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", str(a)).strip()        a = norm(a)        if not a:                                         # pure symbols, e.g. currency sign "؋or "$"            if raw and raw in unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", str(pred)):                return True            continue        if re.fullmatch(r"\d+", a):                       # numbers: whole-token match            if re.search(rf"(?<!\d){a}(?!\d)", p):                return True            continue        if a in p:                                        # exact phrase inside answer            return True        toks = [t for t in a.split() if len(t) > 1]       # "Franklin D. Roosevelt" vs "Franklin Roosevelt"        if len(toks) >= 2 and all(re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(t)}\b", p) for t in toks):            return True    return False
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- promptsdef build_items(data_path, per_category, seed):    data = json.load(open(data_path))["data"]    by_cat = defaultdict(list)    for e in data:        if any(str(e[k][0]).strip() == "" for k in ("A1", "A2", "Answer")):            continue                      # a few items in the dataset have an empty gold answer        by_cat[e["category"]].append(e)    rng = random.Random(seed)    items = []

## 第18页

    for cat in sorted(by_cat):        pool = by_cat[cat]        if per_category and len(pool) > per_category:            pool = rng.sample(pool, per_category)        items.extend(pool)    return items
def clear_question(e):    """'the birthplace of X' usually means a city in English, but the gold answer is about the country.    Make the composed question say 'country' explicitly, like the single-hop Q1 does."""    p = e["person"]    return e["Question"].replace(f"the birthplace of {p}", f"the country where {p} was born")
def ctx_question(e):    """Composed question with the first-hop fact stated up front."""    verb = "on" if e["category"] == "birthdate_uspresident" else "in"    return (f"Background fact: {e['person']} was born {verb} {e['A1'][0]}.\n"            f"Using this fact, answer the question: {clear_question(e)}")
def to_prompt(tok, question):    msgs = [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM}, {"role": "user", "content": question}]    if tok is None:  # dry run        return question    return tok.apply_chat_template(msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,                                   enable_thinking=False)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- maindef main():    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()    ap.add_argument("--model", default=None)    ap.add_argument("--data", default="data/compositional_celebrities.json")    ap.add_argument("--out", default="results/exp1_dry")    ap.add_argument("--per-category", type=int, default=0, help="0 = use all items")    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)    ap.add_argument("--max-tokens", type=int, default=32)    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")    args = ap.parse_args()
    items = build_items(args.data, args.per_category, args.seed)    questions = {}    for e in items:        for q in (e["Q1"], e["Q2"], e["Question"], clear_question(e), ctx_question(e)):            questions.setdefault(q, None)    uniq = list(questions)    print(f"{len(items)} items, {len(uniq)} unique prompts", flush=True)
    t0 = time.time()    if args.dry_run:        # fake model: answers each hop correctly, composed questions correctly 60% of the time        rng = random.Random(1)        gold = {}        for e in items:            gold[e["Q1"]] = e["A1"][0]            gold[e["Q2"]] = e["A2"][0]            gold[e["Question"]] = e["Answer"][0] if rng.random() < 0.6 else "Unknown"            gold[clear_question(e)] = e["Answer"][0] if rng.random() < 0.7 else e["A1"][0]            gold[ctx_question(e)] = e["Answer"][0] if rng.random() < 0.9 else "Unknown"        outs = [str(gold[q]) for q in uniq]    else:        from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams        from transformers import AutoTokenizer        tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model)        llm = LLM(args.model, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85, max_model_len=1024, seed=args.seed)        prompts = [to_prompt(tok, q) for q in uniq]        res = llm.generate(prompts, SamplingParams(temperature=0.0, max_tokens=args.max_tokens))        outs = [r.outputs[0].text.strip() for r in res]    answers = dict(zip(uniq, outs))    print(f"generation took {time.time() - t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
    os.makedirs(args.out, exist_ok=True)    rows = []    with open(os.path.join(args.out, "items.jsonl"), "w") as f:        for e in items:            r = {                "category": e["category"], "person": e["person"], "question": e["Question"],                "gold": e["Answer"],                "pred_q1": answers[e["Q1"]], "pred_q2": answers[e["Q2"]],                "pred_comp": answers[e["Question"]], "pred_clear": answers[clear_question(e)],                "pred_ctx": answers[ctx_question(e)],            }            r["q1"] = is_correct(r["pred_q1"], e["A1"])            r["q2"] = is_correct(r["pred_q2"], e["A2"])            r["comp"] = is_correct(r["pred_comp"], e["Answer"])        # original dataset wording            r["clear"] = is_correct(r["pred_clear"], e["Answer"])      # unambiguous wording            r["ctx"] = is_correct(r["pred_ctx"], e["Answer"])            # "stopped halfway": gave the intermediate answer (hop 1) instead of the final one            r["stop_hop1"] = (not r["clear"]) and is_correct(r["pred_clear"], e["A1"])            r["ctx_echo"] = (not r["ctx"]) and is_correct(r["pred_ctx"], e["A1"])            rows.append(r)            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    def summarize(rs):        n = len(rs)        both = [r for r in rs if r["q1"] and r["q2"]]        s = {            "n": n,            "acc_q1": sum(r["q1"] for r in rs) / n,            "acc_q2": sum(r["q2"] for r in rs) / n,            "acc_comp": sum(r["comp"] for r in rs) / n,            "acc_ctx": sum(r["ctx"] for r in rs) / n,            "n_both_hops_correct": len(both),        }        if both:            k = len(both)            s["gap_original"] = 1 - sum(r["comp"] for r in both) / k   # knows both hops, fails to combine            s["gap_clear"] = 1 - sum(r["clear"] for r in both) / k     # same, unambiguous wording (headline)            s["gap_context"] = 1 - sum(r["ctx"] for r in both) / k     # still fails with hop-1 fact given            s["stopped_at_hop1"] = sum(r["stop_hop1"] for r in both) / k            s["context_echo"] = sum(r["ctx_echo"] for r in both) / k        return s
    by_cat = defaultdict(list)    for r in rows:        by_cat[r["category"]].append(r)    cats = {c: summarize(rs) for c, rs in sorted(by_cat.items())}    # headline: only categories where enough items have both hops right (small cells are too noisy)    MIN_BOTH = 30    kept = [c for c, s in cats.items() if s["n_both_hops_correct"] >= MIN_BOTH]    headline = summarize([r for c in kept for r in by_cat[c]]) if kept else {}    summary = {"model": args.model or "dry-run", "min_both_per_category": MIN_BOTH,               "categories_in_headline": kept, "headline": headline,               "all_items": summarize(rows), "by_category": cats}    json.dump(summary, open(os.path.join(args.out, "summary.json"), "w"), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
    print(f"\n{'category':30s} {'both':>5s} {'gap_orig':>9s} {'gap_clear':>9s} {'gap_ctx':>8s} {'stop@1':>7s}")    for c, s in cats.items():        if s["n_both_hops_correct"]:            print(f"{c:30s} {s['n_both_hops_correct']:5d} {s['gap_original']:9.2f} {s['gap_clear']:9.2f} "                  f"{s['gap_context']:8.2f} {s['stopped_at_hop1']:7.2f}")    h = headline    if h:        print(f"\nGAP ({len(kept)} categories with >= {MIN_BOTH} usable items): "              f"of {h['n_both_hops_correct']} items where both hops are known, "              f"{h['gap_clear']:.1%} fail when composed (original wording: {h['gap_original']:.1%}); "              f"{h['stopped_at_hop1']:.1%} stop at the intermediate answer; "              f"{h['gap_context']:.1%} still fail with hop 1 given in context.")
